# Skrift parity audit — phone, iPad, Mac (read 2026-10-02)

Date 2026-10-02. Commit `fe3c6d97` (branch `claude/session-3-ab263e`), read in worktree `session-3-f90c83`.

**Scope.** Every user-visible behaviour in six areas (Notes list + sidebar; Note screen + editor; Capture / import / share; Books; Settings / sync / export; Recording + search + journal), with its phone, iPad and Mac `file:line`, and whether the three devices share it, run twin copies of it, differ, or lack it. iPad and phone are one target (`SkriftMobile`, universal): an iPad cell is the phone cell unless the code branches on size class or idiom. Sync and verb parity of data (CloudKit fields, verbs per device) is already in [plan/parity.md](../parity.md); this audit does not repeat it.

**Method.** 6 inventory agents (one per area) → 6 compare agents (verdict per row) → 6 adversarial verifiers that re-opened the cited code and corrected or refuted rows → 1 synthesizer (this file). All Sonnet. Read-only: no code, QUEUE.md, SPEC.md or tests were touched, nothing was built or run, and no UI test was started. Rows were not run on a device or simulator; where a row says 'unverified' the verifier could only read the code.

**Counts (rows after verification).**

| area | rows | refuted | missed (found by verifier) |
|---|---|---|---|
| list-sidebar | 118 | 0 | 6 |
| note-editor | 140 | 0 | 8 |
| capture-import-share | 134 | 0 | 10 |
| books | 118 | 0 | 5 |
| settings-sync-export | 130 | 0 | 7 |
| recording-search-journal | 113 | 0 | 7 |
| **total** | **753** | **0** | **43** |

Failed areas: none. Every verifier returned; 0 rows were refuted, 53 were corrected (verdict, scope or cite changed, listed in section 4).

**What the synthesizer checked itself** (beyond the verifiers): opened `ProcessPile.swift:25`, `SourceTaxonomy.swift:53-70`, `SharedContent.swift:39-44`, `Memo+Mobile.swift:35-38`, `CaptureInboxDrainer.swift:630-645`, `SidebarView.swift:370-375`, `NamesStore.swift:177-195`, `NamesListView.swift:201-205`; read SPEC C25, C43, C70, C74, C145, C161, C182, C195, C196, C215, C236, C238-C240 and R36, R58, R79, R88; confirmed every clause and R-row number used in section 5 exists in SPEC.md. All of those matched the verified rows. Every other row rests on the verifier's own reading.

Cell convention: a bare file name (`AppTabView.swift:52`) is the same folder as the first full path in that row or area; `—` means no such code, `n/a` means the surface does not exist on that device. Rows carry `[high]` or `[medium]` at the start of the difference when the verifier rated them that way; unmarked rows are low.

### Top 10 differences, by severity

1. **A locked note is not locked on the Mac, and on several phone surfaces.** Mac list rows show a locked rated note's words; delete, bulk delete and un-lock need no auth; the Way-out shelf and its peek show the body; phone Review rows and `Share note…` show or send it (list-sidebar-72, -87, -88, -116, recsj-096, -110, setexp-96, note-lock-03). C161, R88.
2. **Mac recording has no crash recovery and no disk-full handling.** The phone cuts 60 s segments and rebuilds an interrupted take; `MacRecorder` only logs 'write failed' and has no recovery code at all (recsj-034, -033, -029). Data loss.
3. **`SourceKind.of` cannot classify real phone captures or videos.** It reads a wrapper the phone never writes and a key the phone does not use, so an unrated phone capture projects as an Apple Note on the Mac, a phone video as a voice memo, and the shared test passes only because it seeds the wrong shape (capture-source-01, -06).
4. **A Mac typed note is saved at the click.** Click ✎ and leave: an empty 'Note' row syncs to the phone. The phone creates the note on the first keystroke and drops a blank one (C43, D91) (list-sidebar-23, capture-quick-04, -05).
5. **The same file imported through different doors gives different notes.** Three accept lists (Mac lacks flac, aif, ogg, oga); Files / AirDrop audio dated now on the phone and from the filename on the Mac; PNG/GIF re-encoded to JPEG on the phone only; no One note / N notes chooser in the phone's Files importer (capture-import-18, -28, -16, -08).
6. **Phone and Mac export one note to different files.** The file name comes from the user title on the iPad and the polished title on the Mac; `date:` is the local day on one and a string prefix on the other; the Mac prints dangling `[[img_NNN]]` and writes `![[x]]` for a portfolio note (setexp-90, -91, -93).
7. **The Mac lets you edit an audiobook quote.** The quote is inside one editable text view with no read-only range; C172 says only the ramble edits (note-body-29, books-108).
8. **Search is three matchers and none meets C236.** Phone leaves out the summary, Mac rated and Mac quiet each match a different field set (list-sidebar-32, recsj-053).
9. **A blank polish prompt on the Mac polishes with nothing and syncs as nothing.** The iPad refuses a blank and treats the empty blob as 'default', so two devices polish with different prompts for one value (setexp-48).
10. **Phone-created people never link.** Speaker naming adds a person only if a clip embeds and with `aliases: []`; the phone Add Person sheet skips the default-alias rule and wipes an existing person's aliases; name decisions do not sync between devices (note-speaker-02, setexp-107, note-name-03).

Next after these: Process pile excludes locked notes on the phone against C215 (list-sidebar-26); Connections shows a raw 0.7 on the iPad where the Mac shows 1.0, and the iPad 'Show all' is dead (note-conn-06, -11, R58); Mac Apple Note import is dated to the import moment (capture-import-32); phone person delete has no confirmation (R79).

## 3. Parity table

All non-refuted rows (753). Verdicts: `shared` one implementation in `Skrift_Native/Shared`; `twin-same` two copies that agree today; `twin-different` two copies that differ; `missing-phone` / `missing-ipad` / `missing-mac` absent on that device; `deliberate-asymmetry` backed by SPEC, FEATURES.md or a code comment (the `difference` cell names it).

## 4. Missed findings, corrected rows, refuted rows

### Missed by the inventory, found by the verifiers (43)

**Notes list + sidebar** (6)

- Mac sidebar shows stranded rated memos under the Done chip: SidebarView.swift:759-761 `rows = model.filter == .notRated ? [] : strandedMemos` includes them for Done, while chip counts put them in Needs Work (SidebarView.swift:63-64) and phone ProcessPile.matches(.done) (ProcessPile.swift:51) excludes unprocessed notes. A Done list can contain notes that are not done.
- Fading sweep exists twice over the shared predicate MemoLifecycle.sweepDue: phone FadingSweep.swift:20-44 (repository.softDelete over allMemos) vs Mac MacFadingSweep LifecycleSweepScheduler.swift:15-27 (inline deletedAt/trashSeenAt over live memos). Same outcome, twin loop, not covered by any row (relates to row 89).
- Phone Related rows are not dimmed or quiet: MemosListView.swift:585-596 builds MemoRow without quiet/quietLine, so an unrated semantic hit looks like a rated note; Mac Related memo hits go through quietMemoRow (SidebarView.swift:693-694, :798) and are dimmed.
- Phone MemoFilter.hasPhotosOnly and .place (MemosListView.swift:42-45; MemosListView+Derived.swift:158-159) are checked in matchesFilter but nothing in SkriftMobile sets them since the Filter sheet was removed (grep): dead leftovers of the old filter UI, like the Unsynced chip.
- Mac lock toggle on a quiet row (SidebarView.swift:837,855-859) moves the note out of the list: WayOutRules.unpipelined excludes locked (WayOutRules.swift:40), so locking an unrated note on the Mac makes its row vanish from the Mac list while the phone keeps it visible with a lock glyph. Companion to row 73.
- Mac Process row is always shown and ungated by PolishCenter-style availability, while the iPad hides it when PolishCenter.shared.isAvailable is false (MemosListView+Header.swift:130 vs SidebarView.swift:301); no spec line records this.

**Note screen + editor** (8)

- Compact ⋯ dialog offers 'Add recording' unconditionally (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoDetailView.swift:440) while the nav-bar plus is gated on !isShareCapture (:351) and the iPad menu item likewise (:94-96); a narrow-window iPad share capture can append a recording via the dialog only. Mac: none (no append verb).
- Mac sidebar note menu spells the shared verbs its own way: Menu('Copy') with 'Transcript' and 'Markdown' (Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Sidebar/SidebarView.swift:~1102-1105) vs NoteMenuItem.copyTranscript 'Copy transcript' / copyMarkdown 'Copy as Markdown' (Skrift_Native/Shared/UI/NoteMenu.swift:54-55), and 'Lock'/'Unlock' (SidebarView.swift:837) vs 'Lock note'/'Remove lock' (NoteMenu.swift:51-52). Cross-area (list) but the same drift NoteMenu.swift:14 forbids.
- SPEC C81/D20/R37 (picks sync everywhere) is unmet in both directions: Mac CloudKit ingest ignores Memo.nameResolutionsData (Skrift_Native/Shared/Model/Memo.swift:155-164; no reference in SkriftDesktop non-test code) and the Mac's unlinkedNames/namePicks live only on PipelineFile (NoteDisplayView.swift:384-394). Folded into note-name-03 but the fix is a sync item, not a wording item.
- Phone still shows the 'People in this note' row and sheet (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoPageView.swift:638, 1171-1185, 1214-1244) although SPEC D77/C80 remove it; the 'Someone else…' chip there is the phone's only free-text person creation. Folded into note-name-05.
- Tag-library ranking exists three times: NotesRepository.allTags (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Services/NotesRepository.swift:180-188), TagLibrary.mostUsedFirst (Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/NoteProperties.swift:303-306) and an inline sort in NoteProperties.swift:46-47; TagRules.swift (Shared) has no ranking helper.
- Memo-link title/backlink derivation exists in five places with different title chains: MemoPageView.liveLinkTitle/memoLinkCandidates/recomputeBacklinks (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoPageView.swift:774-792, 718-743), iPad ConnectionsPanel.scanBacklinks (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/ConnectionsPanel.swift:584-606), Mac liveTitle/linkCandidates (Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/NoteDisplayView.swift:333-354) and Mac backlinkScan (Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/ConnectionsPanel.swift:116-129). Candidate for one Shared 'note title for display' helper (C239 lists 'title ladder' as a known twin).
- Phone conversation and quote karaoke paths use Karaoke.activeWordIndex/seekTime (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoDetailSupportTypes.swift:64-65, CaptureQuoteViews.swift:67,83, MemoPageView.swift:900) instead of KaraokeTrack; comment at CaptureQuoteViews.swift:61-62 ('same shared seek lookup a voice-note tap uses') is stale since the monologue path moved to KaraokeTrack.
- Phone and Mac lifecycle backlink detection (MemoLifecycle.backlinkedIDs, Skrift_Native/Shared/Pipeline/MemoLifecycle.swift:82-89) scans memo.transcript only, while Connections' LINKED FROM scans transcript + copyedit (MemoPageView.swift:728-733); a Mac-made link that syncs into the enhancement does not stop a note fading although the Connections panel lists it.

**Capture / import / share** (10)

- SourceKind.of(memo) cannot classify a real phone capture. Shared/Pipeline/SourceTaxonomy.swift:63-70 decodes SharedContent from memo.metadataData, where it expects a wrapper {"sharedContent":{...}} (Shared/Model/SharedContent.swift:39-44). The phone stores the capture in the separate Memo.sharedContentData, bare, not wrapped (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Models/Memo+Mobile.swift:35-38,91; CaptureInboxDrainer.swift:636-644). The unit test passes only because it seeds the wrapped shape into metadataData (SkriftDesktopTests/SourceTaxonomyTests.swift:30-33). Effect: a no-audio capture falls to .appleNote, so MemoNoteProjection.sourceType(for:) (SkriftDesktop/Pipeline/Ingest/MemoNoteProjection.swift:160-166) projects an unrated phone capture as .note and the Mac pane skips CaptureBanner and CaptureSharedContentBlock (Features/Review/NoteDisplayView.swift:277-282); the iPad JournalSidePane.swift:220 shows the wrong glyph. Reading, not run.
- iPad has two Cmd-N bindings: SkriftMobile/App/SkriftApp.swift:243-248 ('New Recording') and Features/MemosList/MemosListView+Header.swift:116 ('New note'). FEATURES.md:126 documents Cmd-N as record, FEATURES.md:61 as new note. Which one wins is unverified.
- Phone Open-in / AirDrop has no rating-and-sender sheet and no filename date. SPEC.md:671-674 (C145) and R24 (SPEC.md:1289) require the same slim sheet and the same date ladder; SkriftMobile/App/AppURLHandler.swift:31-39 goes straight to MemoSaver.importAudio/importVideo. capture-import-08 and -28 cover the chooser and the date, not the sheet.
- A Mac-merged multi-clip note loses its clip paragraph breaks on the phone: the Mac writes clip_manifest.json locally (SkriftDesktop/Pipeline/Ingest/IngestService.swift:282-301) but MacMemoAuthor.author (Pipeline/Ingest/MacMemoAuthor.swift:58-82) builds the Memo with no metadata, while the phone reads MemoMetadata.clipManifest on re-transcribe (SkriftMobile/Features/Recording/MemoSaver.swift:796,969).
- Mac-authored Memos name the original file as audioFilename and attach the audio blob under that name: a video 'clip.mov' ships an m4a blob under pf.filename (MacMemoAuthor.swift:58-82 with IngestService.swift:371,410). Whether the phone materialises or plays it correctly under a .mov name is unverified.
- Duplicate display-title ladders still exist outside C239 twin handling: WayOutRules.displayTitle (SkriftDesktop/Pipeline/WayOutRules.swift:111-122) re-derives Memo.displayTitle(enhancedTitle:) (SkriftMobile/Models/MemoDisplay.swift:22-31); the Mac copy lacks enhancedTitle, NoteSnippet.plain and the capture step.
- SPEC.md:752-758 (C239) says its check is 'the list in plan/twins.md has no unmarked row', but plan/twins.md does not exist (ls plan/). plan/parity.md exists but was not opened for this audit.
- FEATURES.md:377 marks 'Quick note (New Note ...)' Desktop n/a while FEATURES.md:61 and SidebarView.swift:351-375 show the Mac has the compose chip and Cmd-N; capture-entry-01..05 inherit the stale n/a.
- Mac ingestFolder (SkriftDesktop/Pipeline/Ingest/IngestService.swift:662-676) accepts only md/audio/video inside a dropped folder and ignores pictures, while a top-level picture drop is bundled (ingestReport :80-125). A folder of photos is silently dropped with no skipped-file report.
- Mac drop accepts a mix the phone share cannot: any file or folder is passed to IngestService, yet the Mac panel message at SidebarView.swift:193 lists only 'voice memos, audio files, or an Apple Notes folder' (already noted as low), and nothing tells the user that PDFs and text are refused until after the drop (banner visible only inside an open note, NoteDisplayView.swift:183).

**Books (audiobooks)** (5)

- Phone never shows the Mac polish for a quote note (MemoPageView.swift:974-977 macPolish needs memo.captureQuote == nil, so polishedBinding is nil and NoteBodyView edits raw), while the Mac shows sanitised ?? enhancedCopyedit ?? transcript (SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/ReviewHelpers.swift:10, used by NoteBody.swift:70). The same quote note reads raw ramble on the phone and polished, name-linked ramble on the Mac. Not checked against SPEC for intent.
- Quote font size differs: phone CaptureQuoteBlock 15.5pt italic (SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/CaptureQuoteViews.swift, Text(quote).font(.system(size: 15.5))) vs Mac 16pt body italic (SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/NoteBody.swift:37,83; BodyTextView.swift:111). Low.
- FEATURES.md ledger is stale for Mac rows: FEATURES.md:238 lists 'Capture memo rendering (detail)' Desktop n/a, but the Mac renders it (NoteBody.swift:75-117 quoteCard, BodyTextView.swift:897 styleLeadingQuote); the 'Quote protection in enhancement' row (FEATURES.md, n/a phone / yes Mac) omits phone PolishEscrow.swift:19-34.
- Mac duplicate metadata decoder: PhoneMetadata (SkriftDesktop/Pipeline/Export/CompilerBridge.swift:19-35) is a second copy of shared MemoMetadata although Shared/Model is compiled into the Mac (project.yml:84). Folded into books-102 but also a candidate for the new twin-gate.
- Mac note-list quote row also lacks the book chip the phone shows (phone Chip(text: bookCaptionLabel) at MemosListView+Row.swift:115-123) because the Mac source-chip gate is sourceType != .audio (SidebarView.swift:1319); covered only in passing by books-103.

**Settings / sync / export** (7)

- Mac note-actions hasPolish is a twin of shared MemoEnhancement.isProcessed: Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Review/NoteActions.swift:25-39 (steps.enhance == .done || all-three-parts) vs Skrift_Native/Shared/Model/MemoEnhancement.swift:73-85 and Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoDetailView.swift:751-754. Shared comment says 'all three apps read it'; Desktop never does (grep isProcessed SkriftDesktop: none). Drives the Process/Export button on both. (Folded into setexp-69 correction; list as a gate-check target.)
- Mac can lock/unlock only unrated memos from a row context menu (Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Sidebar/SidebarView.swift:837,855-859). No lock or remove-lock action for a rated/pipeline note in NoteActions or NoteDisplayView (NoteDisplayView.swift:163 is a session unlock only). Phone/iPad offer Lock Note / Remove Lock in the note menu (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/MemoDetailView.swift:128,462,717) and list (MemosListView.swift:551). May overlap the note-screen area.
- Mac Names editor delete and phone delete differ from Mac Settings Names card: NamesStore.upsert(canonical:aliases:short:) (Skrift_Native/Shared/Naming/NamesStore.swift:177-195) is a phone-convenience API that overwrites aliases/short for an existing canonical; the shared upsert(_:replacing:) (:200-225) merges on same-name ADD. Two add semantics for the same action (phone Add Person sheet vs PersonEditorView vs Mac editor).
- Phone ObsidianSettingsSection author field is hidden unless a folder is picked AND canProcess (Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/Settings/ObsidianSettingsSection.swift:67-73) but MemoDetailView reads skrift.publish.author at publish time (MemoDetailView.swift:763-768), so an iPad that never opened Settings after picking a folder exports with a blank author; Mac wizard asks for author first-run (SetupWizardView.swift:33-35) while iPad never prompts.
- The Mac sync switch also silently stops prompts, language, vocab and destinations sync (PolishPromptsCloudSync.swift:15, VocabularyCloudSync.swift:16, NamesCloudSync.swift:32), while phone/iPad have no switch and their Settings copy never says these ride the same pipe; only setexp-13/120 cover pieces of this. Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/Features/Settings/SettingsView.swift:186-187.
- FEATURES.md doc drift: the 'Custom vocabulary sync' row (FEATURES.md ~line 292) marks Desktop as not built although Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/App/VocabularyCloudSync.swift exists and Mac Settings edits and pushes the list (SettingsView.swift:554-571). Not a code parity gap, but audit consumers relying on FEATURES.md will misread Mac as missing.
- iPad/phone Polish prompt editor trims blank to default via PolishPromptsStore.store (PolishPromptsStore.swift:~95-100) while the Mac prompt TextEditor has no equivalent: an empty Mac prompt is also pushed to iPad as an empty blob, which the iPad then treats as 'default' (adoptSynced removes the key), so the two devices polish with different prompts for one synced value. Skrift_Native/SkriftDesktop/App/PolishPromptsCloudSync.swift:22-24 vs Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Services/Polish/PolishPromptsStore.swift:60-100. (Consequence of setexp-48.)

**Recording + search + journal** (7)

- Skrift_Native/SkriftMobile/Features/MemoDetail/CaptureVoiceAnnotate.swift:190-193 keeps its own m:ss clock for a recording timer (no h:mm:ss) and bypasses Shared/Recording/RecordingCore.swift:48-54 elapsedLabel, so recsj-008 'shared' holds only for RecordView/Mac. Other phone duration formatters: MemoDetail/MemoDetailView.swift:680, Models/MemoDisplay.swift:47, MemoDetailSupportTypes.swift:215.
- Then vs Now input set differs: phone Services/Embeddings/JournalIndexService.swift:138 uses repository.allMemos() (includes fading, non-canonical rows) while Mac Features/Journal/JournalView.swift:111-117 uses only the live partition (JournalView.swift:94-95). Same card can pick a fading note on the phone only.
- Search over locked notes: phone Models/MemoDisplay.swift:84 (transcript match), Mac Features/Shell/AppModel.swift:90 and Pipeline/WayOutRules.swift:162 match note body text with no lock check, so a locked note can surface by a body word on either app (C161 'never shown without auth'). Runtime display of the row not verified. Shared defect, not a twin difference.
- FEATURES.md:126 says iPad ⌘N = record, but SkriftApp.swift:243-248 (New Recording) and MemosListView+Header.swift:116 (New note) both bind ⌘N; FEATURES.md:53 (search-hit flash Mac ➖) is stale because Mac BodyTextView.swift:213-243 now jumps; SPEC C220 says 'Mac rotates at 7 s' but TranscriptionService.swift:160 uses 20 s. Ledgers disagree with code in three places.
- Phone search is short of C236 (summary, PDF, article text) at Models/MemoDisplay.swift:81-98, independent of the phone/Mac twin difference: even a unified matcher would need these fields added.
- Mac Related-in-search and Connections panel rely on ConnectionsIndexService.isActive only (SidebarView.swift:737-741); phone Related footer (MemoPageView.swift:709-711) and iPad panel gate differently on rated/locked: three gates for one concept (recsj-064 residual, recsj-081).
- iPad record sheet can be dismissed by swipe mid-take (NotesBottomChrome.swift:83-98 form sheet, no interactiveDismissDisabled; RecordView.swift:109 only stops the camera onDisappear). Phone full-screen cover cannot. Fate of the live take after swipe unverified (recsj-015).

### Claims dropped before the table (do not re-raise)

- note-title-02: the claim that the phone title placeholder ignores the polished title for monologues is wrong; `titleBinding` already falls back to `macPolish.title` as real text (MemoPageView.swift:949).
- list-sidebar-37: 'the Mac never calls `warmUp`' is wrong; `ConnectionsPanel.swift:78` warms when a note opens. Only the first-keystroke warm-up on search is missing.
- list-sidebar-68: 'typed notes lose the source chip on the Mac' is wrong for rated rows; they get it. Video imports, book captures and every quiet row do not.
- recsj-064: 'a locked rated note still opens Connections on the Mac' is wrong; the locked path never reaches the inspector overlay (NoteDisplayView.swift:127-137, 227-240).
- capture-import-03: 'the Mac silently skips unsupported files' is wrong; `onSkipped` writes a banner (shown only inside an open note, see capture-import-24).
- note-menu-17, setexp-125, capture-import-32: reclassified, not refuted: the first is partly backed by C40/D159, the other two are single-device bugs, not parity gaps (they are in the dead-code item and the Apple Note date item).

## 5. Ready-to-paste queue items

76 items (63 [auto], 13 [tuur]), numbered P1-P76 as placeholders; renumber with `plan/queue.sh add` when pasting and map `needs:` to the real numbers. Format is QUEUE.md's: `### P# [lane] (todo) title`, `spec:`, `needs:`, `gate+:`, `do:`, `check:`. A `check:` in backticks is a command; every new test class named in a `check:` is created by the item (that is why most carry `gate+: yes`). `[tuur]` items with `do: -` are judgement-only and park for the sitting; their briefs are in the block after the item. Audit row ids are in each `do:` so a worker can open the row.

Lane rule used: a decided behaviour (a SPEC clause, an R-row, or a signed mock) is [auto] even when it draws UI; a new look, a new verb with no mock, or a choice between two defensible rules is [tuur] (mock or decision). Overlaps with QUEUE.md's `Later` list are named in the item. Item sizes follow the 2-plan sizing test; several (P7, P20, P40, P64) are close to the upper limit and should be split by the worker at dispatch if the first commit shows two seams.

Suggested first batch (privacy and data first): P1, P2, P32 (Mac typed note), P39 (SourceKind), P64 (Mac recovery), P58 (prompt blank), P14 (people), P3 (Process pile), P35 (dates). Dependencies among them: P2 needs P1, P35 needs P34.

> **Brief for P10.** Phone `ProcessPile.matches` (ProcessPile.swift:50-51): Needs Work = rated and not yet processed, Done = processed (the iPhone never exports). Mac `AppModel.matchesFilter`: Needs Work = pipeline row not exported plus stranded rated notes, Done = exported (list-sidebar-40, -41). A processed-but-unexported note is Done on the phone and Needs Work on the Mac, and the Mac Done list can hold stranded notes that are not done (list-sidebar missed note). Recommended: Done = processed, on every device; 'exported' becomes the destination row's own state. One sentence in SPEC D135/C61, then the build is one shared predicate (`QueueFilter`).

> **Brief for P11.** (1) Unsynced chip: D148 signed it into the chip row, D68 says drop the filter as dead under CloudKit, and nothing sets `syncStatus = .synced` outside the seeders, so the chip is a no-op on the phone and absent on the Mac (list-sidebar-48). Phone `MemoFilter.hasPhotosOnly` and `.place` are dead too. Recommended: remove the chip and the dead filters, supersede D148's chip line. (2) Status pill: D135 says a pill only while working or broken on all three devices; the Mac shows Queued / Transcribed / Enhancing / Ready / Exported on every rated row (list-sidebar-76). Recommended: keep the Mac dashboard pills and write that into D135 as the one platform difference, or drop them. No code until he picks.

> **Brief for P12.** iPad binds ⌘N twice: the app menu 'New Recording' (SkriftApp.swift:243-248) and the list pencil 'New note' (MemosListView+Header.swift:116); which one wins is unverified; FEATURES.md:61 says new note, :126 says record (list-sidebar-22, capture-quick-02, recsj-037). The Mac has only ⌘N (new note) and ⌥⌘C; no menu command for Record, no ⌘F, no ⌘1-3 surfaces (list-sidebar-24, recsj-036, -052). Recommended: ⌘N = new note on every device; Record = ⇧⌘N; Mac gets ⌘F (search), ⌘1 / ⌘2 for Notes / Review, and a Record menu command. He picks; then it is one `.commands` block per app.

## 6. Proposed gate check: fail any NEW phone/Mac twin of shared logic

Proposed only: nothing below exists in the repo. C239 says the twin audit's check is 'the list in `plan/twins.md` has no unmarked row', and `plan/twins.md` does not exist; this is a mechanical version of that check.

**What counts as a twin** (three signals, all name-based, all from `grep`, no build):

1. `type-twin`: a top-level `struct` / `class` / `enum` / `actor` / `protocol` (declared at column 0, name of 8+ characters) in both `SkriftMobile` and `SkriftDesktop` but not in `Shared`. Column 0 keeps nested `Style`, `Row`, `Kind` out.
2. `shared-redeclared`: a type declared in `Shared` and declared again, same name, in either app.
3. `string-twin`: a user-visible string literal of 28+ characters (starts alphanumeric, contains a space, no interpolation) that appears in two of the three trees. This is the signal that catches copy typed twice, the usual first sign of a rule typed twice.

**Baseline.** `plan/twins.baseline` holds today's twins, one `kind<TAB>key` line each. The check fails only on lines not in the baseline, so it starts green. A deliberate difference is marked on its own line with `// twin-ok: <reason>` and is skipped (that is C239's 'marked as a deliberate platform difference with the reason'). Measured against this tree on 2026-10-02 (dry run from outside the repo, nothing written to it): 25 entries, 12 type-twins (`ConnectionsPanel`, `DiarizationData`, `DiarizationService`, `NamesCloudSync`, `NoteBody`, `PolishPromptsCloudSync`, `RootView`, `SettingsView`, `StatusPill`, `TranscriptionService`, `VocabularyBooster`, `VocabularyCloudSync`), 0 shared-redeclared, 13 string-twins; runs in about 1.7 s.

**Hook.** One line in `gate.sh`, before `xcodegen`, so a new twin fails in seconds instead of after the 3-minute suite:

```zsh
./plan/twin-check.sh || { echo "GATE: RED (new phone/Mac twin — see above)"; exit 1; }
```

`gate.sh` and `plan/` are protected; adding it is the `twincheck` item (P76), a hand-merge. After that, `./plan/twin-check.sh --report` regenerates the readable `plan/twins.md` C239 names.

**Script — `plan/twin-check.sh`** (tested by running a copy from outside the repo: `--baseline` then check gives `ok (25 known, 0 new)`; deleting one baseline line makes it exit 1 and print that line):

```zsh
#!/bin/zsh
# plan/twin-check.sh — fails when a NEW phone/Mac twin of shared logic appears (C239).
#   ./plan/twin-check.sh              check against plan/twins.baseline (exit 1 on any new twin)
#   ./plan/twin-check.sh --baseline   rewrite plan/twins.baseline from the current tree
#   ./plan/twin-check.sh --report     print every current twin, grouped (feeds plan/twins.md)
# A twin is deliberate when its line carries `twin-ok: <reason>` — it is then skipped here.
set -u
export LC_ALL=C   # comm and sort must agree
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
ROOT=Skrift_Native
MOB=$ROOT/SkriftMobile; DESK=$ROOT/SkriftDesktop; SHR=$ROOT/Shared
BASE=plan/twins.baseline
T=$(mktemp -d); trap 'rm -rf "$T"' EXIT

swift_files() {  # app code only: no tests, mocks, build products
  find "$1" -name '*.swift' -not -path '*Tests*' -not -path '*/mocks/*' -not -path '*/build*' -not -path '*/.build/*' -print0
}

# 1. top-level type names (column 0 only, so nested Style/Row/Kind never count), >= 8 chars
types() {  # $1 = dir -> sorted unique names
  swift_files "$1" | xargs -0 grep -hE '^((public|private|fileprivate|internal|final|open|indirect) +)*(struct|class|enum|actor|protocol) +[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9_]{7,}' 2>/dev/null \
    | grep -v 'twin-ok' \
    | sed -E 's/^((public|private|fileprivate|internal|final|open|indirect) +)*(struct|class|enum|actor|protocol) +([A-Za-z0-9_]+).*/\4/' | sort -u
}
# 2. user-visible string literals: >= 28 chars, has a space, not a log/assert/debug line
strings() {
  swift_files "$1" | xargs -0 grep -hE '"[A-Za-z0-9][^"\\]{27,}"' 2>/dev/null \
    | grep -vE 'twin-ok|DevLog|os_log|Logger|print\(|assert|fatalError|precondition|#if DEBUG|accessibilityIdentifier|AppStorage|UserDefaults|NSLocalizedDescription' \
    | grep -oE '"[A-Za-z0-9][^"\\]{27,}"' | grep -E '"[^" ]* [^"]*"' | sort -u
}

types "$MOB"  > $T/t.mob;  types "$DESK" > $T/t.desk;  types "$SHR" > $T/t.shr
strings "$MOB" > $T/s.mob; strings "$DESK" > $T/s.desk; strings "$SHR" > $T/s.shr

{
  comm -12 $T/t.mob $T/t.desk | comm -23 - $T/t.shr | sed 's/^/type-twin\t/'        # A: same type name in both apps, not in Shared
  { comm -12 $T/t.shr $T/t.mob; comm -12 $T/t.shr $T/t.desk; } | sort -u | sed 's/^/shared-redeclared\t/'  # B: a Shared type declared again in an app
  { comm -12 $T/s.mob $T/s.desk; comm -12 $T/s.shr $T/s.mob; comm -12 $T/s.shr $T/s.desk; } | sort -u | sed 's/^/string-twin\t/'  # C: same copy typed in two trees
} | sort -u > $T/now

case "${1:-}" in
  --baseline) cp $T/now "$BASE"; echo "twin-check: baseline written, $(wc -l < $T/now | tr -d ' ') twins -> $BASE"; exit 0 ;;
  --report)   cat $T/now; exit 0 ;;
esac
[[ -f "$BASE" ]] || { echo "twin-check: no $BASE — run ./plan/twin-check.sh --baseline once"; exit 1; }
sort -u "$BASE" > $T/base
comm -13 $T/base $T/now > $T/new
if [[ -s $T/new ]]; then
  echo "TWIN-CHECK: new phone/Mac twin(s) of shared logic — move to Skrift_Native/Shared/ or mark the line 'twin-ok: <reason>':"
  sed 's/^/  /' $T/new | head -40
  exit 1
fi
echo "twin-check: ok ($(wc -l < $T/now | tr -d ' ') known, 0 new)"
```

**False-positive risks.**

- Platform views that are meant to share a name (`RootView`, `SettingsView`) are in the baseline; a future `AppDelegate`-style pair would need a `twin-ok`. Cost: one comment.
- A string that is correct twice (an error sentence both targets must say) fires once; fix by moving it to `SharedCopy` (the right answer) or `twin-ok`.
- Reformatting a string (a changed word) makes a baseline line stale and the changed string a 'new' twin; refresh with `--baseline` in the same commit, which a reviewer sees in the diff.
- Strings split across lines, interpolated, or under 28 characters are not seen; so a short label typed twice passes.

**False negatives (what it cannot see).** A twin under a different name: `PhoneMetadata` in `CompilerBridge.swift` re-decodes the shared `MemoMetadata`, `WayOutRules.displayTitle` re-derives `Memo.displayTitle`, `ProcessPile` versus `pendingFiles`. Those come from body shape, not names. A second, slower pass would normalise each top-level function body (strip names, whitespace, literals) and compare 12-line shingles between the two apps; it was not built because it needs tuning against this tree first and would produce many near-misses. Until then the audit's `twin-same` and `twin-different` rows (section 3) are the list of nameless twins, and the P72 and P73 retire the ones that agree today.

**Not covered on purpose.** Tests, mocks, build products, and the `SkriftShare` / `SkriftWidget` extensions' own files (they are phone-side, so they count as phone code for signal 1 and 3).
